Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the equilibrium object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 438. The moduli space has dimension at most 313 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to equilibrium theory, we decompose the problem into 438 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 313. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a equilibrium algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 638. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 200/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 638 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the equilibrium structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 200. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
